Free-Ranging Cat Predation on Wildlife in East Asia: A Scoping Review of Ecological Evidence and Knowledge Gaps.
Animals : an open access journal from MDPI
Authors: Michelle Zhibing Zhou; Christian S Wright
English Summary This review analyzed free-ranging cat predation in East Asia, finding that evidence is geographically uneven and concentrated in Japan, with significant impacts on threatened species in island ecosystems.
Mandarin Summary (中文摘要) 本研究综述了东亚地区散养猫的捕食行为，发现相关证据在地理分布上不均衡，且主要集中在日本，对岛屿生态系统中的受威胁物种造成了显著影响。
Matched keywords: cat; cats; felis catus

Age-Related Decrease in Interthalamic Adhesion Thickness in Cats Without Apparent Cognitive Dysfunction.
Animals : an open access journal from MDPI
Authors: Réjane Kerbrat-Copy; Océane Thérier; Marion Fusellier
English Summary This study evaluated the relationship between age and interthalamic adhesion thickness in cats, finding a significant negative association where thickness decreases as cats age, particularly in senior life stages.
Mandarin Summary (中文摘要) 本研究评估了猫的年龄与丘脑间粘连厚度之间的关系，发现两者呈显著负相关，即随着猫年龄增长，丘脑间粘连厚度会减小，在老年阶段尤为明显。

The Most Effective Molnupiravir Dosage for Eliminating Feline Calicivirus from Cats With and Without Chronic Gingivostomatitis.
Animals : an open access journal from MDPI
Authors: Kristina Macaulay; Craig Tebeau; Sarah Green; Stewart McSporran; Diane D Addie
English Summary Researchers evaluated molnupiravir dosages for treating feline calicivirus in cats, finding that 40-55 mg/kg daily effectively stopped viral shedding and improved gingivitis symptoms.
Mandarin Summary (中文摘要) 研究人员评估了莫诺拉韦治疗猫杯状病毒的剂量，发现每日40-55毫克/公斤的剂量能有效停止病毒排毒并改善猫的牙龈炎症状。

Animals : an open access journal from MDPI
Authors: Marta López-Alonso; Belén Larrán; Nekane Flores; Inmaculada Orjales; Marta Miranda
English Summary This systematic review examined mineral composition in dog and cat diets, revealing significant variability and frequent mineral deficiencies or excesses across various commercial and alternative diet types.

English Summary This study compared the pharmacokinetics of subcutaneous tolfenamic acid in cats under two different anesthesia protocols, finding that drug absorption and elimination varied depending on the anesthesia used.
Mandarin Summary (中文摘要) 本研究比较了两种不同麻醉方案下猫皮下注射托芬那酸的药代动力学，发现药物的吸收和消除情况会随麻醉方式的不同而产生差异。

Mandarin Summary (中文摘要) 研究人员利用液相色谱-串联质谱法定量分析了收容所猫的粪便糖皮质激素代谢物，发现这些代谢物是评估猫入所后压力水平的有效非侵入性生物标志物。

Animals : an open access journal from MDPI
Authors: Iljin Kim; Jang-Hyuk Yun
English Summary This review examines the comparative psychopharmacology of dogs and cats, noting that while some serotonergic agents are evidence-based for dogs, feline data remains scarce and requires cautious, species-specific clinical application.
Mandarin Summary (中文摘要) 本综述探讨了猫狗的精神药理学比较，指出尽管某些血清素类药物在犬类中有循证支持，但猫的相关数据仍然匮乏，临床应用需谨慎并考虑物种特异性。
